## 7. Termination

_No `Terminate` action. The run ends when the action graph does._

## 8. Error handling and run-time configuration

**Actions wired to run on something other than success.** These are the flow's catch
paths — an action that runs after `Failed`, `Skipped` or `TimedOut` is error handling,
whatever it is named.

| Action | Type | Runs after | On statuses |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Scope_Global/Switch_Action/case:Case_INIT/Scope_INIT_LOAD/SCOPE_INIT/Scope_Primary_Data/Scope_Get_Tasks/Scope_Task_Comments` | `Scope` | `Scope_Tasks_List` | Succeeded, TimedOut, Skipped, Failed |
| `Scope_Global/Switch_Action/case:Case_INIT/Scope_INIT_LOAD/Scope_INIT_ERROR` | `Scope` | `SCOPE_INIT` | Failed, Skipped, TimedOut |
| `Scope_Global/Switch_Action/case:Case_RREFRESH_EMAILS/Scope_REFRESH_EMAILS_ERROR` | `Scope` | `Scope_REFRESH_EMAILS` | Skipped, Failed, TimedOut |
| `Scope_Global/Switch_Action/case:Case_GET_ALL/SCOPE_GET_ALL/Scope_GET_ALL_Primary_Data/Scope_Get_Tasks_1/Scope_Task_Comments_1` | `Scope` | `Scope_Tasks_List_1` | Succeeded, TimedOut, Skipped, Failed |
| `Scope_Global/Switch_Action/case:Case_GET_BOOTSTRAP/Scope_GET_BOOTSTRAP/SCOPE_GET_BOOTSTRAP_PROCESSING/Scope_GET_BOOTSTRAP_Primary_Data_/Scope_Get_Tasks_2/Scope_Task_Comments_2` | `Scope` | `Scope_Tasks_List_2` | Succeeded, TimedOut, Skipped, Failed |
| `Scope_Global/Switch_Action/case:Case_list-activities/Scope_list-activities/Scope__list-activities_Error` | `Scope` | `SCOPE_list-activities_Processing` | Failed, Skipped, TimedOut, Succeeded |
| `Scope_Flow_Data_Capture` | `Scope` | `Scope_Global` | Succeeded, Failed, TimedOut, Skipped |

_No action carries a retry policy, secure-data setting, operation option, tracked
property or loop limit. Every call runs on the platform defaults._

}\n        .label { font-weight: bold; color: #005164; font-size: 13px; display: block; margin-bottom: 2px; }\n        .value { margin-bottom: 10px; display: block; }\n        \n        /* Button */\n        .btn-container { text-align: center;  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"track-style.css\">\n</head>\n<body>\n    <div class=\"container\">\n        <div class=\"header\">\n            <h2>Document Status Tracker</h2>\n            <p>Enter your details to see the real-time status of your submission.</p>\n        </div>\n\n        <form id=\"trackingForm\">\n            <label for=\"trackingID\">Tracking ID</label>\n            <input type=\"text\" id=\"trackingID\" name=\"trackingID\" placeholder=\"e.g., NITDA-DOC-1001\" required>\n\n            <label for=\"email\">Submission Email</label>\n            <input type=\"email\" id=\"email\" name=\"email\" placeholder=\"The email you used to submit\" required>\n\n            <button type=\"submit\" id=\"checkStatusButton\">Check Status</button>\n        </form>\n\n        <div id=\"loader\" class=\"loader-container hidden\">\n            <div class=\"loader\"></div>\n            <p>Checking status...</p>\n        </div>\n\n        <div id=\"statusMessage\" class=\"hidden\"></div>\n        \n        <div id=\"resultsContainer\" class=\"hidden\">\n            <div class=\"result-header\">\n                <h3 id=\"resultTitle\"></h3>\n                <span id=\"resultDocType\" class=\"tag\"></span>\n            </div>\n            <p class=\"result-meta\">\n                Submitted by: <strong id=\"resultSubmitter\"></strong> on <strong id=\"resultDate\"></strong>\n            </p>\n\n            <div class=\"stepper-wrapper\">\n                <div class=\"stepper-item\" data-step=\"Submitted\">\n                    <div class=\"step-counter\"></div>\n                    <div class=\"step-name\">Submitted</div>\n                </div>\n                <div class=\"stepper-item\" data-step=\"In Review\">\n                    <div class=\"step-counter\"></div>\n                    <div class=\"step-name\">In Review</div>\n                </div>\n                <div class=\"stepper-item\" data-step=\"Actioned\">\n                    <div class=\"step-counter\"></div>\n                    <div class=\"step-name\">Actioned</div>\n                </div>\n                <div class=\"stepper-item\" data-step=\"Closed\">\n                    <div class=\"step-counter\"></div>\n                    <div class=\"step-name\">Closed</div>\n                </div>\n            </div>\n        </div>\n    </div>\n    <script src=\"track.js\"></script>\n</body>\n</html>\nComponent 2: track-style.css (The UI/UX Styling)\nCreate this file as track-style.css. This CSS provides the premium, mobile-first feel and contains all the logic for the visual status stepper.\n\nCSS\n\n/* === BASE STYLES === */\nbody {\n    font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, \"Segoe UI\", Roboto, \"Helvetica Neue\", Arial, sans-serif;\n    background-color: #f4f7f6;\n    margin: 0;\n    padding: 20px;\n    display: flex;\n    justify-content: center;\n    align-items: center;\n    min-height: 100vh;\n}\n\n.container {\n    max-width: 600px;\n    width: 100%;\n    margin: 0 auto;\n    background-color: #ffffff;\n    padding: 30px;\n    border-radius: 12px;\n    box-shadow: 0 6px 20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.07);\n}\n\n.header {\n    text-align: center;\n    border-bottom: 2px solid #e0e0e0;\n    padding-bottom: 20px;\n    margin-bottom: 30px;\n}\n\n.header h2 {\n    color: #003366;\n    margin-bottom: 5px;\n}\n\n.header p {\n    font-size: 16px;\n    color: #555;\n}\n\n/* === FORM STYLES === */\nlabel {\n    display: block;\n    margin-bottom: 8px;\n    font-weight: 600;\n    color: #333;\n}\n\ninput[type=\"text\"],\ninput[type=\"email\"] {\n    width: 100%;\n    padding: 14px;\n    margin-bottom: 20px;\n    border: 1px solid #ccc;\n    border-radius: 8px;\n    box-sizing: border-box; \n    font-size: 16px;\n}\n\ninput[type=\"text\"]:focus,\ninput[type=\"email\"]:focus {\n    border-color: #0078d4;\n    outline: none;\n    box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(0,120,212,0.2);\n}\n\nbutton[type=\"submit\"] {\n    width: 100%;\n    background-color: #0078d4;\n    color: white;\n    padding: 15px;\n    border: none;\n    border-radius: 8px;\n    cursor: pointer;\n    font-size: 18px;\n    font-weight: 600;\n    transition: background-color 0.3s ease;\n}\n\nbutton[type=\"submit\"]:hover {\n    background-color: #005a9e;\n}\n\nbutton[type=\"submit\"]:disabled {\n    background-color: #ccc;\n    cursor: not-allowed;\n}\n\n/* === HELPER CLASSES === */\n.hidden {\n    display: none;\n}\n\n/* === LOADER STYLES === */\n.loader-container {\n    text-align: center;\n    padding: 20px 0;\n    color: #555;\n}\n.loader {\n    border: 6px solid #f3f3f3;\n    border-top: 6px solid #0078d4;\n    border-radius: 50%;\n    width: 50px;\n    height: 50px;\n    animation: spin 1s linear infinite;\n    margin: 0 auto 10px auto;\n}\n@keyframes spin {\n    0% { transform: rotate(0deg); }\n    100% { transform: rotate(360deg); }\n}\n\n/* === STATUS/ERROR MESSAGE STYLES === */\n#statusMessage {\n    text-align: center;\n    padding: 15px;\n    margin-top: 20px;\n    border-radius: 8px;\n    font-weight: 500;\n    font-size: 16px;\n}\n.status-error {\n    background-color: #f2dede;\n    color: #a94442;\n    border: 1px solid #ebccd1;\n}\n.status-not-found {\n    background-color: #fff3cd;\n    color: #856404;\n    border: 1px solid #ffeeba;\n}\n\n/* === RESULTS STYLES === */\n#resultsContainer {\n    margin-top: 30px;\n    border-top: 2px solid #e0e0e0;\n    padding-top: 25px;\n}\n.result-header {\n    display: flex;\n    justify-content: space-between;\n    align-items: center;\n    margin-bottom: 10px;\n}\n.result-header h3 {\n    color: #003366;\n    margin: 0;\n}\n.tag {\n    background-color: #e1f5fe;\n    color: #01579b;\n    padding: 5px 10px;\n    border-radius: 15px;\n    font-size: 12px;\n    font-weight: 600;\n}\n.result-meta {\n    font-size: 15px;\n    color: #333;\n    margin-bottom: 30px;\n}\n\n/* === INNOVATIVE STATUS STEPPER === */\n.stepper-wrapper {\n    display: flex;\n    justify-content: space-between;\n    position: relative;\n    margin-bottom: 20px;\n}\n\n.stepper-item {\n    position: relative;\n    display: flex;\n    flex-direction: column;\n    align-items: center;\n    flex: 1;\n}\n\n.stepper-item::before, .stepper-item::after {\n    position: absolute;\n    content: '';\n    border-bottom-width: 3px;\n    border-bottom-style: solid;\n    border-bottom-color: #e0e0e0;\n    top: 15px;\n    width: 50%;\n    z-index: 1;\n}\n\n.stepper-item::before { left: 0; }\n.stepper-item::after { right: 0; }\n\n/* Remove lines for first and last items */\n.stepper-item:first-child::before { display: none; }\n.stepper-item:last-child::after { display: none; }\n\n.step-counter {\n    height: 30px;\n    width: 30px;\n    display: flex;\n    justify-content: center;\n    align-items: center;\n    border-radius: 50%;\n    background: #e0e0e0;\n    border: 3px solid #e0e0e0;\n    color: #ffffff;\n    font-weight: 600;\n    z-index: 2;\n    transition: all 0.3s ease;\n}\n\n.step-name {\n    font-size: 14px;\n    font-weight: 500;\n    margin-top: 10px;\n    color: #777;\n    text-align: center;\n}\n\n/* === Stepper Active/Completed States === */\n\n/* When a step is 'completed' */\n.stepper-item.completed .step-counter {\n    background-color: #28a745; /* Green */\n    border-color: #28a745;\n}\n.stepper-item.completed .step-counter::before {\n    /* Add checkmark */\n    content: '\\2713';\n    font-size: 18px;\n}\n.stepper-item.completed .step-name {\n    color: #000;\n}\n.stepper-item.completed::after {\n    border-bottom-color: #28a745;\n}\n\n/* When a step is 'active' (current step) */\n.stepper-item.active .step-counter {\n    background-color: #ffffff;\n    border-color: #0078d4; /* Blue */\n    color: #0078d4;\n    /* Pulsing animation */\n    animation: pulse 1.5s infinite;\n}\n.stepper-item.active .step-name {\n    color: #0078d4;\n    font-weight: 700;\n}\n.stepper-item.active::before {\n    /* The line leading up to the active step */\n    border-bottom-color: #28a745;\n}\n\n@keyframes pulse {\n    0% { box-shadow: 0 0 0 0 rgba(0, 120, 212, 0.4); }\n    70% { box-shadow: 0 0 0 10px rgba(0, 120, 212, 0); }\n    100% { box-shadow: 0 0 0 0 rgba(0, 120, 212, 0); }\n}\nComponent 3: track.js (The Frontend Logic)\nCreate this file as track.js. This JavaScript handles the form submission, calls the backend, and (most importantly) translates the response into the visual stepper.\n\nJavaScript\n\ndocument.addEventListener(\"DOMContentLoaded\", () => {\n    // --- THIS IS THE ONLY LINE YOU MUST EDIT ---\n    // Paste your NEW Power Automate HTTP POST URL for Tracking here\n    const powerAutomateTrackingUrl = \"PASTE_YOUR_NEW_TRACKING_FLOW_URL_HERE\"; \n    // ------------------------------------------\n\n    const form = document.getElementById(\"trackingForm\");\n    const submitButton = document.getElementById(\"checkStatusButton\");\n    const statusMessage = document.getElementById(\"statusMessage\");\n    const loader = document.getElementById(\"loader\");\n    const resultsContainer = document.getElementById(\"resultsContainer\");\n    const stepperItems = document.querySelectorAll(\".stepper-item\");\n\n    form.addEventListener(\"submit\", async (e) => {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        \n        if (powerAutomateTrackingUrl === \"PASTE_YOUR_NEW_TRACKING_FLOW_URL_HERE\") {\n            showStatus(\"Configuration Error: Tracking Flow URL not set.\", \"error\");\n            return;\n        }\n\n        setLoading(true);\n        clearResults();\n\n        const trackingID = document.getElementById(\"trackingID\").value;\n        const email = document.getElementById(\"email\").value;\n\n        const payload = {\n            trackingID: trackingID,\n            email: email\n        };\n\n        try {\n            const response = await fetch(powerAutomateTrackingUrl, {\n                method: \"POST\",\n                headers: {\n                    \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n                },\n                body: JSON.stringify(payload),\n            });\n\n            if (response.ok) {\n                const data = await response.json();\n                displayResults(data);\n            } else if (response.status === 404) {\n                showStatus(\"No match found. Please check your Tracking ID and Email and try again.\", \"not-found\");\n            } else {\n                showStatus(\"An error occurred while checking the status. Please try again later.\", \"error\");\n            }\n        } catch (error) {\n            console.error(\"Fetch Error:\", error);\n            showStatus(\"A network error occurred. Please check your connection.\", \"error\");\n        } finally {\n            setLoading(false);\n        }\n    });\n\n    function displayResults(data) {\n        // Populate header\n        document.getElementById(\"resultTitle\").textContent = data.title;\n        document.getElementById(\"resultSubmitter\").textContent = data.submitterName;\n        document.getElementById(\"resultDate\").textContent = data.submissionDate;\n        \n        // Populate tag\n        const docTypeTag = document.getElementById(\"resultDocType\");\n        docTypeTag.textContent = data.documentType || \"Document\";\n        \n        // Update the stepper\n        updateStepper(data.status);\n\n        // Show the results\n        resultsContainer.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    }\n\n    function updateStepper(status) {\n        // This logic maps your SharePoint statuses to the 4-step visual\n        // We use numbers to represent the step (1-4)\n        let activeStep = 0;\n\n        switch (status) {\n            case \"Submitted\":\n                activeStep = 1;\n                break;\n            case \"Under Review (EA)\":\n            case \"Forwarded to DG\":\n            case \"Forwarded to Dept\":\n                activeStep = 2;\n                break;\n            case \"Actioned\":\n                activeStep = 3;\n                break;\n            case \"Closed\":\n                activeStep = 4;\n                break;\n            default:\n                activeStep = 0; // Unknown status\n        }\n\n        stepperItems.forEach((step, index) => {\n            const stepNumber = index + 1;\n            if (stepNumber < activeStep) {\n                step.className = 'stepper-item completed';\n            } else if (stepNumber === activeStep) {\n                step.className = 'stepper-item active';\n            } else {\n                step.className = 'stepper-item';\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    function showStatus(message, type) {\n        statusMessage.textContent = message;\n        statusMessage.className = `status-${type}`;\n    }\n\n    function clearResults() {\n        resultsContainer.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n        statusMessage.className = \"hidden\";\n        statusMessage.textContent = \"\";\n        stepperItems.forEach(step => step.className = 'stepper-item');\n    }\n\n    function setLoading(isLoading) {\n        submitButton.disabled = isLoading;\n        loader.classList.toggle(\"hidden\", !isLoading);\n    }\n});\n"
```

Please check your Tracking ID and Email and try again.\", \"not-found\");\n            } else {\n                showStatus(\"An error occurred while checking the status. Please try again later.\", \"error\");\n            }\n        } catch (error) {\n            console.error(\"Fetch Error:\", error);\n            showStatus(\"A network error occurred. Please check your connection.\", \"error\");\n        } finally {\n            setLoading(false);\n        }\n    });\n\n    function displayResults(data) {\n        // Populate header\n        document.getElementById(\"resultTitle\").textContent = data.title;\n        document.getElementById(\"resultSubmitter\").textContent = data.submitterName;\n        document.getElementById(\"resultDate\").textContent = data.submissionDate;\n        \n        // Populate tag\n        const docTypeTag = document.getElementById(\"resultDocType\");\n        docTypeTag.textContent = data.documentType || \"Document\";\n        \n        // Update the stepper\n        updateStepper(data.status);\n\n        // Show the results\n        resultsContainer.classList.remove(\"hidden\");\n    }\n\n    function updateStepper(status) {\n        // This logic maps your SharePoint statuses to the 4-step visual\n        // We use numbers to represent the step (1-4)\n        let activeStep = 0;\n\n        switch (status) {\n            case \"Submitted\":\n                activeStep = 1;\n                break;\n            case \"Under Review (EA)\":\n            case \"Forwarded to DG\":\n            case \"Forwarded to Dept\":\n                activeStep = 2;\n                break;\n            case \"Actioned\":\n                activeStep = 3;\n                break;\n            case \"Closed\":\n                activeStep = 4;\n                break;\n            default:\n                activeStep = 0; // Unknown status\n        }\n\n        stepperItems.forEach((step, index) => {\n            const stepNumber = index + 1;\n            if (stepNumber < activeStep) {\n                step.className = 'stepper-item completed';\n            } else if (stepNumber === activeStep) {\n                step.className = 'stepper-item active';\n            } else {\n                step.className = 'stepper-item';\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    function showStatus(message, type) {\n        statusMessage.textContent = message;\n        statusMessage.className = `status-${type}`;\n    }\n\n    function clearResults() {\n        resultsContainer.classList.add(\"hidden\");\n        statusMessage.className = \"hidden\";\n        statusMessage.textContent = \"\";\n        stepperItems.forEach(step => step.className = 'stepper-item');\n    }\n\n    function setLoading(isLoading) {\n        submitButton.disabled = isLoading;\n        loader.classList.toggle(\"hidden\", !isLoading);\n    }\n});\n"
```

No further action is required from you at this stage.\n                </p>\n            </td>\n        </tr>\n        <tr>\n            <td class=\"footer\">\n                NITDA Digital Ops Automation System\n            </td>\n        </tr>\n    </table>\n\n</body>\n</html>\n\n"

| Field | As provisioned |
|---|---|

| Kind | — |
| Description | _not declared_ |
| Runs after | `Scope_Finalize_Response_State` (Succeeded) |

```json
{
  "ok": "@less(variables('varStatusCode'), 400)",
  "status": {
    "http": "@variables('varStatusCode')",
    "code": "@if(less(variables('varStatusCode'), 400), 'OK', concat('ERR', string(variables('varStatusCode'))))",
    "message": "@if(less(variables('varStatusCode'), 400), 'Success', 'Failed')"
  },
  "request": {
    "requestId": "@variables('varRequestId')",
    "trackingId": "@if(empty(triggerBody()?['trackingId']), coalesce(triggerBody()?['trackingID'], ''), triggerBody()?['trackingId'])",
    "action": "@if(empty(triggerBody()?['action']), 'emailtotaskassignment', triggerBody()?['action'])",
    "operation": "@if(empty(triggerBody()?['operation']), 'create', triggerBody()?['operation'])",
    "mode": "single",
    "requestedBy": "@if(empty(triggerBody()?['userEmail']), coalesce(triggerBody()?['NewActivityTask']?['CreatedBy'], 'unknown'), triggerBody()?['userEmail'])",
    "source": "@if(empty(triggerBody()?['source']), 'http', triggerBody()?['source'])"
  },
  "timing": {
    "receivedAtUtc": "@if(empty(variables('varReceivedAtUtc')), variables('varCompletedAtUtc'), variables('varReceivedAtUtc'))",
    "completedAtUtc": "@variables('varCompletedAtUtc')",
    "durationMs": "@variables('varDurationMs')"
  },
  "data": "@variables('varData')",
  "errors": "@variables('varErrors')",
  "meta": {
    "ts": "@variables('varCompletedAtUtc')",
    "runId": "@workflow()?['run']?['name']",
    "flowName": "@workflow()?['name']",
    "contractVersion": "2026-03-23.5"
  }
}
```

  },

  }
